- **sequencing-data-handling**: Handle biological sequence data in FASTA, FASTQ, VCF, GFF3, BED, and SAM formats. - **sequencing-depth-correlated-dimension-exclusion**: Use when processing sci-L3-RNA/ATAC count matrices with Seurat/Signac and cell assignments must be validated against SNV ground truth — deep-sequenced cells otherwise separate on a depth-driven component. - **sequencing-depth-downsampling**: Use when validating that a Hi-C compartment/sub-compartment inference method recovers stable scores at shallow sequencing depths, by simulating trans-interaction summary files at multiple downsampled depths (e.g. 1x, 0.1x, 0.01x). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-downsampling--2ef7682135974888))
- **sequencing-depth-downsampling**: Use when validating that a broad-domain caller (e.g., RECOGNICER on H3K27me3/H3K9me3 ChIP-seq) is robust to sequencing depth by downsampling mapped reads and comparing aggregate domain length and FRIP across depths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-downsampling--676a88f58b7c11ec))
- **sequencing-depth-downsampling**: Use when benchmarking a long-read ARG profiling or taxonomic classification pipeline's sensitivity to sequencing depth, requiring matched subsets of reads spanning low (1–2×) to high (32×) coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-downsampling))
- **sequencing-depth-downsampling-simulation**: Decision support for whether a ChIP-seq/DNase-seq QC metric like FRiP is a genuine quality readout or merely an artifact of sequencing depth, especially when benchmarking shallow libraries or choosing downsampling conventions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-downsampling-simulation))
- **sequencing-depth-fold-change-simulation**: Use when validating that a single-cell cluster-purity metric (e.g., ROGUE) is insensitive to sequencing-depth fold changes between replicates or to variation in cell number, by simulating replicate mixtures and comparing ROGUE/S against silhouette. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-fold-change-simulation))
- **sequencing-depth-matched-model-selection**: Use when scoring/predicting chromatin loops on a new Hi-C, Micro-C, or DNA SPRITE contact map (.cool/.hic) in a cell type or species different from the training set, and a depth-matched pre-trained model must be chosen. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-matched-model-selection))
- **sequencing-depth-normalization**: Use when computing normalized ChIP-seq signal (buildChIPprofile, recoverChIPlevels, meta-plots, boxplots) from BAM files of samples sequenced at different depths, or when down-sampling to test depth requirements. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-normalization))
- **sequencing-depth-normalization-comparison**: Use when pairwise metagenome distances must resolve communities differing in sequencing depth, dominant-organism abundance, or genetic composition, and MinHash sketches (Mash) or presence/absence metrics (Simka Jaccard) compress or saturate those differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-normalization-comparison))
- **sequencing-depth-perturbation-analysis**: Use when a single-cell velocity model's outputs must be shown stable under technical noise, specifically when perturbing raw count matrices by downsampling to reduced sequencing-depth proportions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-perturbation-analysis))
- **sequencing-depth-rarefaction-control**: Use when validating a longitudinal differential-abundance method against sequencing-depth bias: you need negative-control data with no true time effect but varying library sizes, or must quantify FPR inflation from compositional normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-rarefaction-control))
- **sequencing-depth-regression-normalization**: Use when a SnapATAC cell-by-cell Jaccard kernel must be normalized for per-cell sequencing-depth bias before spectral dimensionality reduction of scATAC-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-regression-normalization))
- **sequencing-depth-robustness-analysis**: Use when evaluating loop-calling model robustness on downsampled Hi-C contact maps, or when comparing graph-view vs image-view branch performance across sequencing depths (e.g., 1.5% / 39M reads up to 520M reads). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-robustness-analysis))
- **sequencing-depth-saturation-analysis**: Use when you have a high-coverage dRNA-seq dataset (e.g. PromethION human reads, SRA BioProject PRJNA995902) and need to quantify how sequencing depth affects m6A tool performance (hits, F1, AUPRC). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-saturation-analysis))
- **sequencing-depth-saturation-analysis**: Use when planning or justifying sequencing depth for shotgun metatranscriptomes (e.g., human gut stool samples), or when asked to demonstrate via subsampling how many annotated reads are required to reach a target accuracy for low-abundance transcripts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-saturation-analysis))
- **sequencing-depth-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when you need to quantify how a viral metagenomic classifier/reconstruction pipeline (e.g. VirMAP on VMC reads) degrades as sequencing depth is reduced, or to compare retention of a reconstruction pipeline against a standard read-classification baseline at shallow depth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-sensitivity-analysis))
- **sequencing-depth-stratified-recall-evaluation**: Use when a large single-cell dataset with deposited predefined cell labels is available and you need to quantify whether per-cell UMI counts (sequencing depth) bias the accuracy of an automatic annotation tool such as deCS.correlation(). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-stratified-recall-evaluation))
- **sequencing-depth-subsampling-assessment**: Use when evaluating whether richness estimates and rarefaction curves from finished DADA2 ASV tables can be trusted, especially for mock community data subsampled across sequencing depths (e.g. 100–1,600,000 read pairs or 100–40,000 reads). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-subsampling-assessment))
- **sequencing-depth-variance-simulation**: Use when evaluating how an estimator (e.g., allelic proportion or expression estimates) performs at reduced sequencing depth, by simulating low-coverage data via random per-cell read down-sampling from full-depth counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-variance-simulation))
- **sequencing-error-correction**: Use when exact-overlap-based read recruitment (e.g., TAR-VIR overlap extension with an overlap cutoff) is planned downstream, since raw sequencing errors would break exact overlap matching between reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-correction))
- **sequencing-error-injection-simulation**: Use when benchmarking tandem-repeat decomposition accuracy against a known ground truth, e.g. simulating (AC)m(AG)n-style mosaic TRs with substitution error injection before scoring predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-injection-simulation))
- **sequencing-error-markov-chain-modeling**: Use when benchmarking isoform quantification methods (e.g. Longcell, Sicelore2, FLAMES, wf-single-cell, IsoQuant) on simulated Nanopore reads where per-read error processes must be modeled at controlled low/middle/high quality regimes with a ground-truth cell-by-isoform matrix for scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-error-markov-chain-modeling))
- **sequencing-error-metrics-tabulation**: Use when comparing simulated nanopore read quality across simulators (e.g. seq2squiggle vs squigulator) on R10.4.1 datasets and you need median match rate and median PHRED score per organism/mode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-metrics-tabulation))
- **sequencing-error-model-configuration**: Use when you must generate simulated shotgun metagenomic FASTQ benchmarks at prescribed mean depths with an instrument-specific error model (NovaSeq) for validating variant-discovery tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-model-configuration))
- **sequencing-error-modeling-gene-calling**: Use when gene-calling on unassembled metagenomic reads from error-prone platforms (e.g. 454 with homopolymer-run indels), where frameshifts in raw reads break standard gene predictors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-modeling-gene-calling))
- **sequencing-error-probability-modeling**: Use when assembling overlapping Illumina paired-end reads (e.g. 16S rRNA amplicons in FASTQ/SAM) where naive perfect-match overlap discards ~40% of reads due to uncalled or miscalled bases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-probability-modeling))
- **sequencing-error-rate-benchmarking**: Use when raw PacBio CLR read sets (e.g., yeast pseudo-diploid, NWC metagenome, Microbial 10-plex) must be corrected and their residual sequencing error rate computed to reproduce or compare against published correction benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-rate-benchmarking))
- **sequencing-error-rate-estimation**: Use when you have parent sequences and their one-mismatch children from amplicon fastq data and need to estimate the Illumina substitution error rate or verify that one-mismatch child accumulation follows a Poisson process. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-rate-estimation))
- **sequencing-error-rate-estimation-by-pass-number**: Use when quantifying PacBio CCS read accuracy across circular-consensus pass numbers, or when deciding how many CCS passes are needed before full-length 16S sequence variants can be trusted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-rate-estimation-by-pass-number))
- **sequencing-error-rate-quantification**: Use when error-prone NGS reads (e.g., 454 Titanium pyrosequencing of HIV-1 subtype B pol) from a genetically heterogeneous sample must be error-corrected before estimating haplotype frequencies or clone prevalence.
